Dear Shareholders,

On  behalf  of  the  board  (the  ' Board ')  of  directors  (the ' Directors ')  of  ELL  Environmental  Holdings  Limited  (' ELL Environmental '  or  the  ' Company ',  together  with  its subsidiaries, the ' Group ' or ' we ' or ' our '), I present the annual report  of  the  Company  for  the  year  ended  31  December  2022 (the ' Year ').

According to the data published by China's National Bureau of Statistics,  China's  gross  domestic  product  for  2022  was RMB121.0 trillion 1 ,  representing  a  3.0%  increase  over  last  year. Despite the ongoing impact of COVID-19 on China's economy in 2022, the nation's economy experienced an organised recovery this  year,  establishing  a  strong  basis  for  further  economic recuperation  in  2023.  This  was  achieved  through  the  efficient implementation of targeted policies and measures, with the full cooperation of all regions and departments nationwide, and the successful containment of the epidemic. In recent years, China has  made  significant  efforts  to  advance  its  environmental protection  industry.  According  to  the  National  Bureau  of Statistics,  investment  in  water  conservancy,  environment  and public  facilities  management  increased  by  10.3% 2   in  2022 compared to  the  same  period  in  the  previous  year.  In  October 2022,  China's  Ministry  of  Ecology  and  Environment  released  a report  on  national  surface  water  and  ambient  air  quality  for January  to  September  2022,  and  among  the  3,641  national surface  water  assessment  sections,  the  proportion  of  sections with  excellent  water  quality  (Grade  I~III)  reached  86.3% 3 , representing an increase of 4.5% compared to the same period i n  the  previous  year.  For  the  seven  major  river  basins, Northwestern Rivers, Southwestern Rivers and rivers in Zhejiang and  Fujian  Provinces,  the  proportion  of  sections  with  excellent water  quality  (Grade  I~III)  reached  88.5% 3 ,  representing  an increase of 4.8% compared to the same period in the previous year.  This  highlights  the  Chinese  government's  unwavering dedication  to  addressing  environmental  issues,  including wastewater management, even amidst economic challenges and

致各位股東：

本人謹代表強泰環保控股有限公司 （ 「 強 泰環保 」 或 「 本公司 」 ，連同其附屬公司， 統稱 「本集團」 或 「 我們 」 ） 董 事 （ 「 董事 」 ） 會 （ 「 董事會 」 ） 呈 報 截 至⼆零⼆ ⼆年十⼆月 三十⼀日止年度 （ 「 本年度 」 ） 的全年報告。

根據中國國家統計局數據顯示，⼆零 ⼆⼆年全年中國國內生產總值為人民幣 121.0 萬億元 1 ，比去年同期增長 3.0% 。 儘管新冠肺炎疫情在⼆零⼆⼆年不斷沖 擊國內的經濟，在全國各地區、各部門 的通⼒合作下，種種針對性的政策及措 施得以有效地落實，疫情因而受到有效 的控制，使全國經濟今年有序復甦，為 ⼆零⼆三年經濟的進⼀步恢復打下堅實 的基礎。近年來中國大⼒推動國內環保 業，據國家統計局數據顯示，⼆零⼆⼆年 全年水利、環境和公共設施管理業投資 額比去年同期增長 10.3% 2 。根據生態環 境部於⼆零⼆⼆年十月公佈的全國地表 水環境質量狀況，本年度⼀至九月，於 3,641 個國家地表水考核斷面中，水質優 良 （ Ⅰ∼Ⅲ類） 斷面比例為 86.3% 3 ，比去年 同期高出 4.5% 。而七大流域及西北諸河、 西南諸河和浙閩片河流水質優良 （Ⅰ∼Ⅲ類） 斷面比例則為 88.5% 3 ，比去年同期上升 4.8% 。由此可見，中國政府在處理經濟 問題、使經濟恢復向好的同時，仍致⼒ 於處理污水及環境等問題。針對長江及 黃河為中國兩條主要河道，政府亦對其

efforts to revive the economy. The Chinese government has also established  distinct  guidelines  and  specific  targets  for  the Yangtze  River  and  Yellow  River,  which  are  two  crucial  rivers  in the  country.  In  August  2022,  17  departments  and  units, including the Ministry of Ecology and Environment, the National Development and Reform Commission, the Ministry of Housing and  Urban-Rural  Development,  and  the  Ministry  of  Water Resources,  jointly  issued  the  Action  Plan  for  Deepening  the Battle  for  the  Protection  and  Restoration  of  the  Yangtze  River 4 ( 《深入打好長江保護修復攻堅戰行動方案》 ),  which proposed a  number  of  major  targets  to  be  achieved  by  the  end  of  2025, focusing  on  the  Yangtze  River  and  its  surrounding  areas, including maintaining the water quality of Yangtze River Basin at an  excellent  level,  maintaining  the  water  quality  of  the  main stream  at  'Grade  II',  tackling  and  eliminating  the  problem  of mixing  and  misconnecting  sewage  networks  in  cities  at  the prefecture  level  and  above;  and  improving  the  centralised collection rate of urban domestic sewage to more than 70%, or more  than  5  percentage  points  higher  than  that  of  2020.  In addition,  navigation-restricted  and  prohibited  areas  shall  be designated  in  important  aquatic  habitats  of  the  Yangtze  River, aquatic  biological  monitoring  system  should  be  established  for the  Yangtze  River,  and  the  closed  fishing  areas  should  be regulated, in order to achieve restorative growth for the aquatic biological  resources  in  the  Yangtze  River.  As  for  the  Yellow River,  another  major  river  in  our  country,  12  departments including the Ministry of Ecology and Environment jointly issued the Action Plan for Protecting and Managing the Ecology of the Yellow  River 5   ( 《黃河生態保護治理攻堅戰行動方案》 )  in  the second half  of  2022,  with  the  primary  objective  to  improve  the pollution control and water quality of the Yellow River, while also striving  for  continuous  and  steady  improvement  in  the ecosystem quality of the Yellow River basin. The actual target is that by 2025, the water quality in the upper and middle reaches of the mainstream of the Yellow River should reach Grade II, the elimination rate of black and odorous water bodies in the builtup  areas  of  county-level  cities  should  reach  more  than  90%, and  no  less  than  90%  of  the  drinking  water  in  cities  at  county level  and  above  should  have  the  water  quality  of  Class  III  or above.  The  Yellow  River  basin  covers  nine  provinces  and regions,  including  Shanxi,  Inner  Mongolia,  Henan  and Shandong.  The  primary  focus  of  action  should  be  directed towards the ecology of the main tributaries, main streams, and significant lakes and reservoirs within the basin. Moving forward, the Group plans to remain updated on the most current national

分別發出不同指引及訂下相關目標。⼆零 ⼆⼆年八月，生態環境部、國家發展和 改革委員會、住房和城鄉建設部、水利 部等多達 17 個部門和單位聯合發佈《深入 打好長江保護修復攻堅戰行動方案》 4 ， 提出於 2025 年年底前，針對長江及其周 邊的⼀些主要目標，其中括長江流域 的水質需保持優良，幹流水質需保持為 Ⅱ類，基本處理和消除地級及以上城市 污水管網混錯接問題，城市生活污水集 中收集率需提升至 70% 以上或高於 2020 年的 5 個百分點以上。另外，於長江水生 生物重要栖息地劃定禁止及限制航行的 區域、建立長江水生生物監測體系及管 理禁漁水域，使長江水生生物資源恢復 性增長。至於另⼀條主要河道 -黃河， 生態環境部等 12 個部門於⼆零⼆⼆年下 半年亦聯合發佈《黃河生態保護治理攻 堅戰行動方案》 5 ，當中主要目標為提升 黃河的污染治理及水質水平，黃河流域 的生態系統質量需持續及穩定地得到提 升。實際指標為於 2025 年，黃河幹流上 中游的水質需達到Ⅱ類，縣級城市建成 區黑臭水體消除比例達到 90% 以上，縣 級及以上城市飲⽤水水質達到Ⅲ類或以 上的比例不可低於 90% 。當中黃河流域 的覆蓋範圍為⼭西、內蒙古、河南、⼭東 等 9 個省區，而主要⽀流、幹流、重要湖 庫的生態為關注的重點。本集團未來仍

and  regional  policies  and  actively  collaborate  to  make  a contribution towards national environmental governance efforts.

As  the  comprehensive  implementation  of  environmental policies  in  China  presents  growth  opportunities,  the  Group  is also  proactively  addressing  challenges  in  the  environmental industry  abroad.  To  improve  our  competitiveness  in  overseas markets, the Group currently focuses on two overseas projects, including  the  development  of  biofuel  pellet  business  in  Jambi, Indonesia  and  the  development  of  a  power  supply  project  in Bangka,  Indonesia.  The  biofuel  pellets  business  has  been  put into production and generated revenue since 2021. Construction is  currently  underway  for  the  Bangka  power  plant  project  in Indonesia. The Group has entered into an agreement with PLN to  supply  electricity  to  its  national  grid  at  a  fixed  price  for  a period of 25 years from 2023.

Other than the above two overseas key projects, the Group has been  endeavouring  to  explore  opportunities  to  communicate with  other  power  generation  plants  and  companies  to  seek for  more  cooperation.  The  Group  expects  to  develop  more environmental  protection  businesses  in  the  future,  continue researching  for  the  development  opportunities  of  other  new energy  businesses  in  the  environmental  protection  industry, thereby stabilising the Group's business foundation and allowing the  Group  to  achieve  diversified  business  and  sustainable development.  In  terms  of  the  Group's  domestic  businesses, the  Group  will  continue  to  enhance  its  operation  efficiency  by strictly  controlling  cost  and  improving  operational  process, so  as  to  further  enhance  the  profitability  of  the  wastewater treatment business. The management will continue to align with national policies, guiding the Group towards a pathway of good development.

For  the  year  ended  31  December  2022,  ELL  Environmental recorded  a  revenue  of  approximately  HK$203.6  million  with  a year-on-year  increase  of  97.5%.  The  gross  profit  for  the  Year was  HK$60.6  million,  representing  a  year-on-year  increase  of 12.9%. The net loss was HK$2.2 million.

<!-- image -->

會繼續跟隨國家及地區的最新政策，加 以配合，致⼒為國家的環境治理工作作 出貢獻。

國內環保政策的全面推行無疑為本集團 帶來增長的機遇，與此同時，本集團亦 積極地因應國外環保業帶來的挑戰作出 應對。為提高本集團在國外市場的競爭 ⼒，本集團現時主要集中發展兩項國外 項目，括於印尼佔碑省發展生物燃料 球團的業務，以及於印尼邦加島發展供 電項目。本集團的生物燃料球團業務已 於⼆零⼆⼀年投產並開始產生收入。至 於印尼邦加島發電廠項目方面，邦加島 發電廠正處於建造階段，本集團已與印 尼國家電⼒公司達成供電協議，預期由 ⼆零⼆三年起為期⼆十五年，以固定價格 向國家電網進行供電。

除以上兩項國外重點項目外，本集團⼀ 直致⼒探索與其他發電廠及企業溝通的 機會，以尋求合作的空間。本集團希望 未來可以開展更多環保業務項目，繼續 研究環保產業中其他新能源業務的發展 機遇，穩扎集團業務基礎，使集團達至 業務多元性及可持續發展。針對本集團 的國內業務，本集團將繼續嚴格控制成 本、改良營運流程以提升運作效率，進 ⼀步提升污水處理業務帶來的盈利。管 理層將會繼續配合國家的政策，引領集 團走向良好的發展道路。

截至⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日止年度， 強泰環保錄得營業收入約 203.6 百萬港元， 同比增長 97.5% 。本年度毛利為 60.6 百萬 港元，同比增長 12.9% 。淨虧損為 2.2 百萬 港元。

<!-- image -->

Despite  the  recurrence  of  COVID-19  outbreaks  in  2022  and uncertainties  in  the  global  economic  landscape,  the  Group remains  committed  to  upholding  its  established  operational principles  and  proactively  addressing  market  challenges.  On one  hand,  we  will  continuously  optimise  the  management  of existing  projects  to  bring  stable  growth  for  the  Group.  On  the other  hand,  we  will  keep  a  close  eye  on  market  changes  and actively  cooperate  with  the  latest  national  and  regional  policies to maintain the steady development of the wastewater treatment business in China to provide clean water for the whole country. In  addition,  the  Group  will  explore  appropriate  and  promising opportunities  to  extend  its  environmental  philosophy  overseas and contribute to global sustainable development by expanding its range of diverse environmental businesses.

Here, I would like to express my gratitude to my fellow Directors, shareholders  of  the  Company  (the  ' Shareholders '),  staff  and stakeholders  for  their  constant  support.  Thanks  to  the  tireless dedication  of  the  management  and  all  staff,  as  well  as  the support  from  our  customers  and  partners,  ELL  Environmental has  been  able  to  overcome  various  obstacles  in  such  a challenging  business  environment  in  recent  years.  The  Group will continuously maintain its high-quality service level and close relationship  with  customers,  with  the  aim  to  contribute  to  the society  and  environment,  as  well  as  create  value  and  generate long-term stable returns for our investors and the Shareholders.

CHAU On Ta Yuen Chairman

Hong Kong, 30 March 2023

疫情在⼆零⼆⼆年內持續反覆，外圍經 濟環境存在不確定性，然而，本集團將 繼續堅守過往的經營理念，積極應對市 場上的各種挑戰，⼀方面持續優化現有 項目的管理，為本集團帶來穩定的增長， 另⼀方面，本集團將密切留意市場變化， 並積極配合國家及地區的最新政策，在 中國污水處理業務上繼續穩步發展，為 全國上下提供潔淨的⽤水，同時尋求合 適及具發展潛⼒的機遇，透過發展多元 化的環保業務，把本集團的環保理念帶 至海外，為全球的可持續發展出⼀份⼒。

在此本人向各位董事、股東、員工及各 持份者⼀直以來的⽀持致以衷心感謝。 有賴管理層及全體員工⼀直以來的不辭 勞苦，加上客戶和合作夥伴的⽀持，強 泰環保才能在近年嚴峻的營商環境下跨 過重重挑戰。本集團會繼續保持優質的 服務水平，以及與客戶維持緊密的合作 關係，為社會及環境作出貢獻，並為投 資者及股東創造價值，帶來長期穩定的 回報。

主席 周安達源 香港，⼆零⼆三年三月三十日

## BUSINESS REVIEW

Due  to  external  factors  such  as  international  geopolitical changes  and  the  outbreak  of  Omicron,  a  variant  of  the COVID-19, coupled with internal factors such as the downturn in the  domestic  real  estate  market,  China's  overall  economic growth was under pressure in 2022. However, with the support of  the  Chinese  government's  various  'economy  stabilisation' policies,  the  economy  grew  steadily  throughout  the  Year, demonstrating the resilience of the China's economy. According to  the  data  published  by  the  National  Bureau  of  Statistics, China's gross domestic product for 2022 was RMB121.0 trillion 1 , representing a 3.0% growth over 2021.

In  recent  years,  the  Chinese  government has taken a proactive approach  to  water  pollution  issues.  The  Chinese  government has established distinct guidelines and specific targets for  the two  major  rivers  in  China  that  cover  vast  areas,  the  Yangtze River  and  the  Yellow  River,  in  order  to  create  a  more comprehensive wastewater treatment system and improve water quality. In August 2022, 17 departments and units, including the Ministry of Ecology and Environment, the National Development and  Reform  Commission,  the  Ministry  of  Housing  and Urban-Rural Development and the Ministry of Water Resources, jointly  issued  the  Action  Plan  for  Deepening  the  Protection  and Restoration of the Yangtze River *  ( 《深入打好長江保護修復攻堅 戰行動方案》 ) 2 ,  which  proposed  a  number  of  major  targets  to be achieved by the end of 2025 focusing on the Yangtze River and its  surrounding  areas,  which  include  maintaining  the  water quality  in  the  Yangtze  River  basin  at  an  excellent  level, maintaining the water quality of the mainstream of Yangtze River at  Grade  II,  tackling  and  eliminating  the  problem  of  mixing  and misconnecting sewage networks in cities at the prefecture level and  above,  and  improving  the  centralised  collection  rate  of urban  domestic  sewage  to  more  than  70%  or  more  than  five percentage  points  higher  than  that  of  2020.  In  addition, navigation-restricted and prohibited areas should be designated in  important  aquatic  habitats  of  the  Yangtze  River,  aquatic biological  monitoring  system  should  be  established  for  the Yangtze River, and the closed fishing areas should be regulated, in  order  to  achieve  restorative  growth  for  the  aquatic  biological

## 業務回顧

由於外圍因素如國際地緣政治變化以及 新冠肺炎的奧米克戎變種病毒的爆發， 再加上內在因素如國內房地產市場的下 行，中國整體經濟的增長在⼆零⼆⼆年 面對不少壓⼒，但在中央政府各項 「穩經 濟」 政策的⽀持下，全年經濟穩中有升， 展現了中國經濟的韌性。根據國家統計 局的數據顯示，⼆零⼆⼆年全年中國國內 生產總值為人民幣 121.0 萬億元 1 ，比⼆零 ⼆⼀年同期增長 3.0% 。

近年，政府對水污染問題採取積極應對 態度，而長江及黃河作為中國兩條主要 河道，覆蓋範圍甚廣，故政府對兩條河 道分別發出不同指引及訂下相關目標， 以打造更完善的污水處理系並改善水質。 ⼆零⼆⼆年八月，生態環境部、國家發展 和改革委員會、住房和城鄉建設部、水 利部等多達 17 個部門和單位聯合發佈《深 入打好長江保護修復攻堅戰行動方案》 2 ， 提出於⼆零⼆五年年底前，針對長江及 其周邊的⼀些主要目標，其中括長江 流域的水質需保持優良，幹流水質需保 持為Ⅱ類，基本處理和消除地級及以上 城市污水管網混錯接問題，及城市生活 污水集中收集率需提升至 70% 以上或高 於⼆零⼆零年的 5 個百分點以上。另外， 於長江水生生物重要棲息地劃定禁止及 限制航行的區域，建立長江水生生物監 測體系，及管理禁漁水域，使長江水生 生物資源恢復性增長。至於另⼀條主要 河道 -黃河，生態環境部等 12 個部門於

resources in the Yangtze River. As for the Yellow River, another major  river  in  China,  12  departments,  including  the  Ministry  of Ecology  and  Environment,  jointly  issued  the  Action  Plan  for Protecting and Managing the Ecology of the Yellow River *  ( 《黃河 生態保護治理攻堅戰行動方案》 ) 3   in  the  second  half  of  2022, with  the  primary  objective  to  improve  the  pollution  control  and water  quality  of  the  Yellow  River,  while  also  striving  for continuous and steady improvement in the ecosystem quality of the  Yellow  River  basin.  The  actual  target  is  that  by  2025,  the water quality in the upper and middle reaches of the mainstream of the Yellow River should reach Grade II, the elimination rate of black and odorous water bodies in the built-up areas of countylevel cities should reach more than 90%, and no less than 90% of  the  drinking  water  in  cities  at  county  level  and  above  should have  the  water  quality  of  Grade  III  or  above.  The  Yellow  River basin  covers  nine  provincial  regions  including  Shanxi,  Inner Mongolia, Henan and Shandong, with the focus of action should be  directed  towards  the  ecology  of  the  main  tributaries, mainstream  and  important  lakes  and  reservoirs.  It  is  apparent that  China  and  various  departments  pay  high  attention  to  the water quality of the Yangtze River and the Yellow River, and are committed to implementing a series of measures to achieve the relevant  targets.  According  to  the  national  surface  water environment quality status announced by the Ministry of Ecology and Environment in  October  2022,  from  January  to  September this  Year,  among  the  3,641  national  surface  water  assessment sections, the proportion of sections with excellent water quality (Grade I-III) reached 86.3% 4 , which was 4.5% higher than that of the same period of 2021, and the proportion of inferior sections with Grade V was 0.9%, which was 0.3% lower than that of the same  period  of  2021.  In  the  future,  it  is  expected  that  more manpower  and  funds  will  be  devoted  by  China  in  dealing  with water  quality  issues,  and  the  relevant  index  will  continue  to improve steadily.

Overall,  the  Group  has  been  committed  to  prudently  operating its  existing  businesses  and  exploring  the  potential  of  other environmental  protection  businesses  in  the  past.  Earlier,  the Group sold  the  relevant  interests  in  Haian  Hengfa  Wasterwater Treatment  Company  Limited  (' Haian  Hengfa '),  and  no  longer held  a  wastewater  treatment  facility  operated  by  it,  with  the

整體而言，本集團於過去致⼒於審慎地 營運現有的業務，並開發其他環保業務 的可能性。早前，本集團已出售海安恆發 污水處理有限公司 （ 「 海安恆發 」 ） 的 相 關 權益，亦不再持有其營運的⼀座污水處 理設施，相關的資源將會被重新分配至

relevant resources to be reallocated to the wastewater treatment facilities  operated  by  Rugao  Hengfa  Water  Treatment  Company Limited  (' Rugao  Hengfa ')  and  the  power  plant  project  in Bangka,  Indonesia  (the  ' Bangka  Project ').  After  the  disposal of  Haian  Hengfa,  the  Group  will  mainly  operate  the  wastewater treatment  facilities  of  Rugao  Hengfa  in  China.  In  July  2021, the  Group  reached  an  agreement  with  the  local  administrative committee,  increasing  water  tariffs  from  RMB2.67  per  tonne to  RMB3.43 per tonne, contributing to a more robust result for the  Group.  With  the  increase  in  water  tariffs  and  improvement in  project  efficiency,  the  management  expects that the revenue and gross profit of the Group will further increase.

The Group has also been actively preparing for the development of  suitable  environmental  protection  projects  in  overseas  and two  key  projects  are  currently  being  developed.  Among  which, the  Bangka  Project  is  still  under  construction  but  has  reached a  consensus regarding the electricity selling plan with the local government  and  entered  into  the  power  supply  agreement with  PT  Perusahaan  Listrik  Negara  (Persero)  (also  known  as State  Electricity  Company),  an  Indonesian  government-owned corporation,  and  is  expected  to  supply  power  to  the  national grid  at  a  fixed  price  for  25  years  since  2023.  If  the  Bangka Project  turns  out  to  be  a  success,  we  expect  that  the  relevant operation  model  can  be  replicated  in  other  outlying  islands  in Indonesia.  Moreover,  we  will  also  continue  our  study  on  the feasibility  of  developing  other  new  energy  investment  projects in  other  regions  of  Indonesia,  while  actively  promoting  the 'Build-Operate-Transfer'  or  'BOT'  investment  projects  in  order to  generate  greater  value  for  the  Group  and  bring  better  cash flows and investment return.

As  for  the  biofuel  pellet  business  in  Indonesia,  the  Group  has been  actively  expanding  such  business  and  continuing  to explore  opportunities  to  cooperate  with  other  power  plants, thereby  promoting  and  developing  the  biofuel  pellet  business. The  Group's  biofuel  pellet  business  in  Indonesia  commenced its  wood  pellet  production  business  in  2021  and  has  been generating  revenue.  With  the  development  of  biofuel  pellet business,  the  management  is  confident  that  the  biofuel  pellet business  will  provide  growth  momentum  for  the  Group  and enable the Group to achieve diversification of its environmental businesses in the future.

## Management Discussion and Analysis

## 管理層討論與分析

由如皋恆發水處理有限公司 （ 「 如皋恆發 」 ) 營運的污水處理設施以及印尼邦加島發 電廠的項目 （ 「 邦加島項目 」 ） 。在出售海安 恆發後，本集團於國內將主⼒營運如皋 恆發的污水處理設施，本集團與當地的 管委會於⼆零⼆⼀年七月達成的協議，水 價由每噸人民幣 2.67 元增加至每噸人民 幣 3.43 元，此將為本集團帶來更加穩健 的業績，而伴隨水價上升及項目效能提升， 管理層期望本集團的收入及毛利將會進 ⼀步提升。

本集團在國外亦⼀直積極籌備發展合適 的環保項目，現時主要發展兩個重點項目。 其中邦加島項目正處於建造階段，本集 團已與當地政府談妥售電方案，並與印 尼國有公司 PT Perusahaan Listrik Negara (Persero) （⼜稱國家電⼒公司） 達成供電協 議，預期自⼆零⼆三年起為期⼆十五年， 以固定價格向國家電網供電。若果邦加 島電廠項目取得成功，預期相關營運模 式可複製至印尼其他離島進行。除此之 外，亦可繼續研究於印尼其他地區發展 其他新能源投資項目的可行性，同時積 極推進 「建設 -經營 -移交」 或 「 BOT 」 投 資項目，務求為本集團締造更多價值， 帶來更理想的現金流量及投資回報。

印尼生物燃料球團業務方面，本集團⼀ 直積極開拓該業務並繼續探索與其他發 電廠合作的機會，推動及發展生物燃料 球團的業務。印尼生物燃料球團業務已 於⼆零⼆⼀年開展生產木顆粒，並開始 產生收入。隨著生物燃料球團的發展， 管理層有信心生物燃料球團業務將帶動 本集團增長，於將來亦有助本集團達至 環保業務多元化的發展目標。

## FINANCIAL REVIEW

## REVENUE

Our  total  revenue  increased  by  HK$100.5  million  or  97.5%  to HK$203.6 million  for  FY2022  as  compared  to  HK$103.1  million for  FY2021.  Such  increase  was  primarily  attributable  to  (i)  the increase  in  construction  revenue  arising  from  the  construction works of the Bangka Project during FY2022, and (ii) the increase in  sales  of  biofuel  in  Indonesia  during  FY2022  as  compared  to FY2021.

## COST OF SALES

Our total cost of sales increased by HK$93.6 million or 189.1% from  HK$49.5  million  for  FY2021  to  HK$143.1  million  for FY2022,  primarily  due  to  (i)  the  increase  in  construction  costs arising from the Bangka Project during FY2022 as compared to FY2021, and (ii)  the  increase  in  the  cost  of  biofuel  in  Indonesia during FY2022 as compared to FY2021.

## GROSS PROFIT AND GROSS PROFIT MARGIN

Our  gross  profit  increased  by  HK$7.0  million  or  12.9%  from HK$53.6  million  for  FY2021  to  HK$60.6  million  for  FY2022, primarily  due  to  the  reasons  of  the  aforementioned  fluctuation in revenue and cost of sales. Our gross profit margin decreased from 52.0% for FY2021 to 29.7% for FY2022.

求精益求精。本集團將繼續配合國家及 地區的最新政策，繼續堅守在中國污水 處理業務的穩固根基，向客戶提供高水 準的服務，為國家締造潔淨水質。本集 團亦會繼續靈活面對市場變化，主動尋 求適合及具發展潛⼒的機遇，把握並創 造更多商機，發展全球多元化的環保業 務，務求貫徹始終，進⼀步為環保盡⼒， 同時促進本集團業務的增長，堅持集團 ⼀貫的理念，為客戶提供優良的服務品質， 共同構建更美好的將來。

## 財務回顧

## 營業收入

我們的營業收入總額由⼆零⼆⼀財政年 度的 103.1 百萬港元增加 100.5 百萬港元或 97.5% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 203.6 百萬 港元。有關增長主要是由於 (i) ⼆零⼆⼆財 政年度邦加島項目的建設工程導致建築 營業收入增加；及 (ii) ⼆零⼆⼆財政年度於 印尼的生物燃料銷售較⼆零⼆⼀財政年 度增加。

## 銷售成本

我們的銷售成本總額由⼆零⼆⼀財政年 度的 49.5 百萬港元增加 93.6 百萬港元或 189.1% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 143.1 百萬 港元，主要是由於 (i) ⼆零⼆⼆財政年度邦 加島項目所產生的建設成本較⼆零⼆⼀ 財政年度增加；及 (ii) ⼆零⼆⼆財政年度於 印尼的生物燃料成本較⼆零⼆⼀財政年 度增加。

## 毛利及毛利率

我們的毛利由⼆零⼆⼀財政年度的 53.6 百 萬港元增加 7.0 百萬港元或 12.9% 至⼆零 ⼆⼆財政年度的 60.6 百萬港元，主要是由 於如上文所述營業收入及銷售成本不穩 定所致。我們的毛利率由⼆零⼆⼀財政 年度的 52.0% 下降至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 29.7% 。

## OTHER INCOME AND NET GAINS

Our  other  income  and  net  gains  increased  slightly  by  HK$0.5 million  or  10.9%  from  HK$4.5  million  for  FY2021  to  HK$4.9 million for FY2022, primarily due to the foreign exchange gain.

## ADMINISTRATIVE EXPENSES

Our  administrative  expenses  increased  by  HK$3.0  million  or 10.9%  from  HK$27.5  million  for  FY2021  to  HK$30.5  million  for FY2022. Administrative expenses mainly include staff costs and other operating expenses.

## FINANCE COSTS

Our  finance  costs  increased  by  HK$4.9  million  or  110.9% from  HK$4.4  million  for  FY2021  to  HK$9.3  million  for  FY2022, primarily due to the increase in loan borrowing for the financing of the Bangka Project during FY2022.

## PROFIT BEFORE TAX

Our  profit  before  tax  decreased  by  HK$8.9  million  or  38.7% from HK$22.9 million for FY2021 to HK$14.0 million for FY2022, primarily due to the factors mentioned above.

## INCOME TAX EXPENSE

Our income tax expense increased by HK$4.8 million or 42.5% from HK$11.4 million for FY2021 to HK$16.2 million for FY2022, mainly  due  to  withholding  tax  incurred  from  the  disposal  of  a subsidiary.

## (LOSS)/PROFIT FOR THE YEAR ATTRIBUTABLE TO OWNERS OF THE COMPANY

(Loss)/profit  attributable  to  owners  of  the  Company  decreased by  HK$9.4  million  or  153.6%  from  profit  of  HK$6.1  million  for FY2021  to  loss  of  HK$3.3  million  for  FY2022,  primarily  due  to the factors mentioned above.

<!-- image -->

## Management Discussion and Analysis 管理層討論與分析

## 其他收入及淨收益

我們的其他收入及淨收益由⼆零⼆⼀財 政年度的 4.5 百萬港元輕微增加 0.5 百萬港 元或 10.9% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 4.9 百萬 港元，主要是由於產生匯兌收益。

## 行政開⽀

我們的行政開⽀由⼆零⼆⼀財政年度的 27.5 百萬港元增加 3.0 百萬港元或 10.9% 至 ⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 30.5 百萬港元。行政 開⽀主要括員工成本及其他經營開⽀。

## 融資成本

我們的融資成本由⼆零⼆⼀財政年度的 4.4 百萬港元增加 4.9 百萬港元或 110.9% 至⼆ 零⼆⼆財政年度的 9.3 百萬港元，主要是 由於⼆零⼆⼆財政年度為邦加島項目融 資而借入的貸款增加。

## 除稅前溢利

我們的除稅前溢利由⼆零⼆⼀財政年度 的 22.9 百萬港元減少 8.9 百萬港元或 38.7% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 14.0 百萬港元，主 要由上文所述原因所致。

## 所得稅開⽀

我們的所得稅開⽀由⼆零⼆⼀財政年度 的 11.4 百萬港元增加 4.8 百萬港元或 42.5% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的 16.2 百萬港元，主 要是由於出售附屬公司產生預扣稅。

## 本公司擁有人應佔年內 （虧損） ╱溢利

本公司擁有人應佔 （虧損） ╱溢利由⼆零 ⼆⼀財政年度的溢利 6.1 百萬港元減少 9.4 百萬港元或 153.6% 至⼆零⼆⼆財政年度 的虧損 3.3 百萬港元，主要由上文所述原 因所致。

## Management Discussion and Analysis 管理層討論與分析

## LIQUIDITY, FINANCIAL AND CAPITAL RESOURCES

Our  principal  liquidity  and  capital  requirements  primarily  relate to  investments  in  our  projects,  construction  and  upgrading  of our  wastewater  treatment  facilities,  purchases  of  equipment as  well  as  costs  and  expenses  related  to  the  operation  and maintenance  of  our  wastewater  treatment  and  electricity generating facilities.

As at 31 December 2022, the Group's bank balances and cash was  HK$34.6  million,  representing  a  decrease  of  46.8%  as compared with that of HK$65.1 million as at 31 December 2021. As at 31 December 2022, the Group's bank balances and cash of  HK$13.0 million, HK$1.8 million, HK$0.9 million and HK$18.9 million  were  denominated  in  Renminbi  (' RMB '),  Hong  Kong Dollars  (' HK$ '),  Indonesian  Rupiah  (' IDR ')  and  United  States Dollars  (' US$ '),  respectively  (31  December  2021:  HK$35.8 million, HK$4.9 million, HK$7.7 million and HK$16.7 million were denominated in RMB, HK$, IDR and US$, respectively).

## BORROWINGS

As at 31 December 2022, the total amount of our utilised bank borrowings  was  HK$38.0  million,  of  which  HK$18.0  million was  repayable  on  demand  and  HK$20.0  million  was  repayable within 2 to 5 years. All HK$38.0 million of the outstanding bank borrowings  were  denominated  in  HK$  (31  December  2021: HK$18.0  million  and  HK$2.2  million  were  denominated  in  HK$ and US$, respectively).  Out  of  the  HK$38.0  million  outstanding bank  borrowings,  HK$18.0  million  bore  a  floating  interest  rate of 1.4% per annum over the Hong Kong Interbank Offered Rate and HK$20.0 million bore a fixed interest rate of 2% below Hong Kong  Dollar  prime  rate  (31  December  2021:  HK$18.0  million bore  a  floating  interest  rate  of  1.4%  per  annum  over  the  Hong Kong Interbank Offered Rate and HK$2.2 million bore a floating interest  rate  of  1.4%  per  annum over London Interbank Offered Rate).  We  had  HK$4.7  million  unutilised  banking  facilities  as  at 31 December 2022 (31 December 2021: HK$7.3 million).

As  at  31  December  2022,  the  Company  had  an  outstanding borrowing  from  independent  third  parties  of  the  Group amounted  to  HK$23.9  million  (31  December  2021:  HK$14.9 million),  which  bore  a  fixed  interest  rate  of  8%  per  annum.  The loan was repayable within one year.

## 流動資金、財務及資本資源

我們主要的流動資金及資本需求主要涉 及我們的項目投資、建設及升級污水處 理設施、購買設備以及與經營及維護污 水處理及發電設施有關的成本及開⽀。

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本集團銀 行結餘及現金為 34.6 百萬港元，較⼆零 ⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日的 65.1 百萬港元減 少 46.8% 。於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日， 本集團的銀行結餘及現金 13.0 百萬港元、 1.8 百萬港元、 0.9 百萬港元及 18.9 百萬港 元分別以人民幣（ 「 人民幣 」 ） 、港元 （ 「 港 元 」 ） 、印尼盾 （ 「 印尼盾 」 ） 及 美 元 （ 「 美元 」 ） 計值 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 35.8 百萬港元、 4.9 百萬港元、 7.7 百萬港元及 16.7 百萬港元分別以人民幣、港元、印尼 盾及美元計值） 。

## 借款

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，我們已動 ⽤的銀行借款總額為 38.0 百萬港元，其 中 18.0 百萬港元須按要求償還， 20.0 百萬 港元須於⼆至五年內償還。全部 38.0 百 萬港元之未償還銀行借款以港元計值 （⼆ 零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 18.0 百萬港元 及 2.2 百萬港元分別以港元及美元計值） 。 在 38.0 百萬港元的未償還銀行借款中， 18.0 百萬港元按香港銀行同業拆息利率 之浮動利率每年 1.4% 計息， 20.0 百萬港元 按港元最優惠利率減 2% 的固定利率計息 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 18.0 百萬港 元按香港銀行同業拆息加 1.4% 的浮動年 利率計息及 2.2 百萬港元按倫敦銀行同業 拆息加 1.4% 的浮動年利率計息） 。於⼆零 ⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，我們的未動⽤銀 行融資為 4.7 百萬港元 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月 三十⼀日： 7.3 百萬港元） 。

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本公司 來自本集團獨立第三方的未償還借款為 23.9 百萬港元 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 14.9 百萬港元） ，按固定利率每年 8% 計息。 該筆貸款須於⼀年內償還。

As  at  31  December  2022,  the  Company  had  outstanding amounts  due  to  related  parties  of  the  Group  amounted  to HK$105.5 million (31 December 2021: HK$39.2 million), of which HK$82.0 million bore a fixed interest rate of 8% per annum and HK$23.5 million bore a fixed interest rate of 6% per annum. Out of  the  HK$105.5  million  outstanding  amounts  due  to  related parties,  HK$90.9  million  was  repayable  within  one  year  and HK$14.6  million  was  repayable  within  two  to  five  years.  To  the best knowledge of the Directors and management of the Group, all  the  interest  rates  of  the  above  borrowings  were  determined on arm's length basis.

## GEARING RATIO

Gearing  ratio  is  calculated  by  dividing  total  debt  by  total equity  and  then  multiplied  by  100%,  and  total  debt  includes the  interest-bearing  bank  borrowings,  amounts  due  to  related parties  and  other  borrowings.  Our  gearing  ratio  increased  from 19.4%  as  at  31  December  2021  to  55.3%  as  at  31  December 2022.

## CHARGE ON ASSETS

As at 31 December 2022, the Group did not have any charge on assets (31 December 2021: nil).

## TREASURY POLICIES

The  Group  has  adopted  a  prudent  financial  management approach  towards  its  treasury  policies  and  thus  maintained a  healthy  liquidity  position  throughout  the  Year.  The  Board closely  monitors  the  Group's  liquidity  position  to  ensure  that the liquidity structure of the Group's assets, liabilities and other commitments  can  meet  its  funding  requirements  from  time  to time.  Surplus  cash  will  be  invested  appropriately  so  that  the Group's cash requirements for its strategy or direction from time to time can be met.

## CAPITAL EXPENDITURES

Our  capital  expenditures  consist  primarily  of  expenditures  for construction for our Bangka Project. During the Year, our capital expenditures amounted to HK$106.1 million (31 December 2021: HK$24.2 million), which were funded by our financing activities.

## 管理層討論與分析

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本公司應 付本集團關聯方未償還款項為 105.5 百萬 港元 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 39.2 百 萬港元） ，其中 82.0 百萬港元按固定利率 每年 8% 計息， 23.5 百萬港元按固定利率 每年 6% 計息。在應付關聯方的未償還款 項 105.5 百萬港元中， 90.9 百萬港元須於 ⼀年內償還及 14.6 百萬港元須於⼆至五年 內償還。據董事及本集團管理層所深知， 上述借款的所有利率均按公平原則釐定。

## 資產負債比率

資產負債比率按債務總額除以權益總額 並乘以 100% 計算，而債務總額括計息 銀行借款、應付關聯方款項及其他借款。 我們的資產負債比率由於⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆ 月三十⼀日的 19.4% 上升至於⼆零⼆⼆年 十⼆月三十⼀日的 55.3% 。

## 資產抵押

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本集團 並無任何資產抵押 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月 三十⼀日：無） 。

## 庫務政策

本集團已對其庫務政策採取審慎的財務 管理方針，故於本年度⼀直維持健康的 流動資金狀況。董事會緊密監察本集團 的流動資金狀況以確保本集團的資產、 負債及其他承擔的流動資金結構可應付 其不時的資金需求。本集團將適當地投 資盈餘現金，以致可不時滿足其策略或 方針的現金需求。

## 資本開⽀

我們的資本開⽀主要括邦加島項目的 建設開⽀。於本年度，我們的資本開⽀為 106.1 百萬港元 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀ 日： 24.2 百萬港元） ，由我們的融資活動 提供資金。

<!-- image -->

## Management Discussion and Analysis 管理層討論與分析

## FOREIGN EXCHANGE RISK

Individual member companies in Mainland China and Indonesia within  our  Group  have  limited  foreign  currency  risk  as  most of  the  transactions  are  denominated  in  the  same  currency  as the  functional  currency  of  the  operations  in  which  they  relate. However, as these principal subsidiaries mainly carry assets and liabilities  in  RMB  and  IDR,  any  appreciation  or  depreciation  of HK$ against RMB and IDR will affect the Group's consolidated financial  position  which  is  presented  in  HK$,  and  will  be reflected in the exchange fluctuation reserve.

The Group does not have a foreign currency hedging policy. The Group  minimises  foreign  exchange  exposure  by  converting  its cash  and  cash  equivalents  in  other  currencies  generated  from the operation of its foreign operating subsidiaries to HK$.

## CONTINGENT LIABILITIES

As at 31 December 2022, the Group had no material contingent liabilities (31 December 2021: nil).

## EMPLOYEE AND REMUNERATION POLICIES

As  at  31  December  2022,  the  Group  had  185  employees  (31 December  2021:  122  employees).  Employee  costs,  including Directors'  emoluments,  amounted  to  approximately  HK$14.6 million  for  FY2022  (FY2021:  HK$14.7  million).  The  remuneration policy  for  our  Directors,  senior  management  members  and general staff is based on their experience, level of responsibility and  general  market  conditions.  Any  discretionary  bonus  and other  merit  payments  are  linked  to  the  profit  performance  of our  Group  and  the  individual  performance  of  our  Directors, senior  management  members  and  general  staff.  The  Group encourages the self-development of its employees and provides on-the-job training where appropriate.

The Company adopted a share option scheme on 5 September 2014  for  the  purpose  of  providing  incentives  and  rewards  to eligible directors and employees of the Group.

## 外匯風險

由於本集團內中國內地及印尼各成員公 司大部份交易採⽤的貨幣與其業務有關 功能貨幣相同，因此此等公司僅承受有 限的外幣風險。但是，由於此等主要附 屬公司的資產及負債主要以人民幣及印 尼盾記賬，港元兌人民幣及印尼盾的任 何升值或貶值將對本集團的綜合財務狀 況 （其以港元呈列） 產生影響並反映於匯 兌波動儲備。

本集團並無外幣對沖政策。本集團透過 將其海外營運附屬公司的營運產生的其 他貨幣的現金及現金等價物轉換為港元， 以盡量減低外匯風險。

## 或然負債

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本集團 並無重大或然負債（⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月 三十⼀日：無） 。

## 僱員及薪酬政策

於⼆零⼆⼆年十⼆月三十⼀日，本集團共 有 185 名 （⼆零⼆⼀年十⼆月三十⼀日： 122 名） 僱員。⼆零⼆⼆財政年度的僱員成本 （括董事酬金）約為 14.6 百萬港元 （⼆零 ⼆⼀財政年度： 14.7 百萬港元） 。我們的董 事、高級管理層成員以及⼀般員工的薪 酬政策乃根據彼等的經驗、所負責任及 ⼀般市場情況釐定。任何酌情花紅及其 他獎勵金均與本集團溢利表現及董事、 高級管理層成員以及⼀般員工的個人表 現掛鈎。本集團鼓勵其僱員自我發展， 並提供適當的在職培訓。

本公司已於⼆零⼀四年九月五日採納⼀項 購股權計劃，以激勵及獎勵本集團合資 格董事及僱員。

## SIGNIFICANT INVESTMENTS, MATERIAL ACQUISITIONS AND DISPOSALS

## 重大投資、重大收購及出售事項

On  21  July  2022,  Everbest  Water  Treatment  Development Company  Limited,  an  indirect  wholly-owned  subsidiary  of  the Company  as  the  seller  (the  ' Seller '),  and  CGN  Environmental Protection Industry Co., Ltd. ( 中廣核環保產業有限公司 )  as  the purchaser (the ' Purchaser '), entered into the sale and purchase agreement  (the  ' Sale  and  Purchase  Agreement '),  pursuant to which the Seller agreed to sell, and the Purchaser agreed to acquire,  70%  of  the  issued  share  capital  of  Haian  Hengfa  (the ' Target Company '), at the total consideration of approximately RMB68.2 million (inclusive of applicable tax) in accordance with the terms and conditions of the Sale and Purchase Agreement. After  the  completion, the Company ceased to hold any interest in  the  Target  Company  and  the  Target  Company  ceased  to  be a subsidiary of the Company. For further details, please refer to the announcement of the Company dated 21 July 2022 and the circular  of  the  Company  dated  29  August  2022  regarding  the disposal of Haian Hengfa.

Save  as  disclosed  above,  the  Group  did  not  have  any  other significant  investments,  material  acquisitions  or  disposals  of assets,  subsidiaries,  associates  or  joint  ventures  during  the Year.

於⼆零⼆⼆年七月⼆十⼀日，本公司間接 全資附屬公司恆發水務發展有限公司 （作 為賣方） （ 「 賣方 」 ） 與 中廣核環保產業有限 公司 （作為買方） （ 「 買方 」 ） 訂立買賣協議 （ 「 買 賣協議 」 ） ，據此，根據買賣協議的條款及 條件，賣方同意出售而買方同意收購海 安恆發 （ 「 目標公司 」 ） 已發行股本的 70% ， 總代價約為人民幣 68.2 百萬元 （括適⽤ 稅項） 。於完成後，本公司不再持有目標 公司的任何權益，而目標公司不再為本 公司的附屬公司。有關進⼀步詳情，請 參閱本公司日期為⼆零⼆⼆年七月⼆十⼀ 日的公告及本公司日期為⼆零⼆⼆年八月 ⼆十九日的通函，內容有關出售海安恆 發。

除上文所披露者外，本集團於本年度並 無任何其他重大投資、重大收購或出售 資產、附屬公司、聯營公司或合營企業。

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## ABOUT THIS REPORT

ELL  Environmental  Holdings  (' ELL  Environmental '  or  the ' Company ',  together  with  its  subsidiaries,  collectively  referred to as the ' Group ', ' we ', ' us , or ' our ') is presenting its seventh Environmental,  Social,  and  Governance  (' ESG ')  Report  to  its stakeholders  to  illustrate  the  Group's  sustainable  development strategy, approach, and accomplishments during the year.

## REPORTING PERIOD AND SCOPE

Unless  otherwise  stated,  the  reporting  scope  of  this  Report covers the Group's major operations from 1 January 2022 to 31 December 2022 (the ' Reporting Period ') of:

- Operation  services  of  the  Rugao  Hengfa  Wastewater Treatment  Facility,  in  Jiangsu  Province,  the  People's Republic of China (' PRC '); and
- Biofuel  pellet  production  business  of  PT  Rimba  Palma Sejahtera Lestari (' RPSL ') in Jambi, Indonesia.

Due  to  completion  of  disposal  of  Haian  Hengfa  Facility  in December 2022, it was excluded in the reporting scope.

## REPORTING STANDARDS

## 關於本報告

- 位於中華人民共和國 （ 「 中國 」 ） 江 蘇 省的如皋恆發污水處理設施的營運 服務；以及

## OUR BUSINESS

Founded  in  2002,  ELL  Environmental  is  a  one-stop environmental  solutions  service  provider.  Currently,  we  offer  a one-stop  approach  to  the  provision  of  wastewater  treatment services  using  the  'Build  -  Operate  -  Transfer'  (or  BOT) model. We have two wastewater treatment facilities in operation which  are  all  located  within  Jiangsu  Province,  China,  treating municipal  and  industrial  wastewater.  The  Group  also  investing in  biofuel  pellet  production  business  in  Jambi,  Indonesia  and developing a power supply business in Bunga Island, Indonesia.

## WASTEWATER TREATMENT

The Group's primary wastewater treatment operations consist of two facilities located in Rugao County, Jiangsu Province, China, which  treat  both  municipal  and  industrial  sewage.  Our  facilities are  equipped  with  Moving  Bed  Biofilm  Reactor  (' MBBR ') treatment  technology  and  have  the  capacity  to  treat  up  to 80,000 tonnes of wastewater daily. The diagrams below provide an overview of the key wastewater treatment processes utilized by our facilities:

## 污水處理

本集團的主要污水處理業務於中國江蘇 省如皋縣擁有⼀座設施，處理生活和工 業污水。設施採⽤流動式接觸床廢水生 物膜反應 （ 「 MBBR 」 ） 處理方法處理污水， 每日可處理量達到 80,000 公噸的污水。 下圖概述設施的主要污水處理流程：

<!-- image -->

Our  wastewater  treatment  facilities  adhere  to  local  wastewater treatment  standards  to  ensure  that  the  treated  wastewater meets quality  requirements.  The  treated  wastewater  undergoes a series of procedures, including:

<!-- image -->

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report

環境、社會及管治報告

我們的污水處理設施均符合當地污水處 理標準，確保污水經處理後符合品質標 準。污水處理的程序括：

1. 過濾

2. 沉澱

3. 調節

4. 酸化

5. 厭氧

6. 殺菌

1. Filtration

2. Sedimentation

3. Adjustment

4. Acidification

5. Anaerobic

6. Sterilization

Our  wastewater  treatment  business  is  committed  to  reducing the  pollution  of  surrounding  water  bodies  by  ensuring  that  the quality  of  treated  wastewater  meets  the  Integrated  Wastewater Discharge Standard and the Class 1-A or Class 1-B Discharge Standard  of  Pollutants  for  Municipal  Wastewater  Treatment Plants  (GB18918-2002).  We  obtain  Pollutant  Emission  Permits as required by the Measures for Pollutant Discharge Permitting Administration  (for  Trial  Implementation).  We  monitor  the  water quality  on  a  daily  basis  and  record  details  such  as  chemical oxygen  demand  (' COD '),  biological  oxygen  demand  (' BOD '), pH  value,  suspended  solids  (' SS '),  ammonia  (NH3),  water content,  organic  materials,  and  pH  value  of  sludge,  etc.  in  a monthly  report.  We  also  conduct  regular  inspections  to  ensure the  quality  of  treated  wastewater  before  it  is  discharged.  Our core responsibility and mission are to maintain the highest level of environmental protection and sustainability.

## BIOFUEL PELLET PRODUCTION

ELL Environmental's subsidiary, RPSL, primarily operates biofuel pellet production businesses in Jambi, Indonesia. The core raw material  used  in  the  production  process  is  wood  pellets,  which are  typically  generated  from  the  production  of  wood  products. Our goal is to maximize the use of wood pellets to help minimize waste  and  reduce  the  burden  on  the  environment.  We  have completed  the  construction  of  a  production  facility  to  process wood  pellets  as  the  major  raw  material,  and  has  commenced operations and sales of processed wood pellets to power plants in Southeast Asia, our primary target customers, in mid-2022.

我們的污水處理業務致⼒保障污水經處 理後的質量，以減少污水對周邊水體所 造成的污染，以符合《污水綜合排放標 準》 及 《城鎮污水處理廠污染物排放標準》 (GB18918 - 2002 ） 的 1-A 級或 1-B 級要求。 我們已取得《排污許可管理辦法 （試行） 》 所要求的《排污許可證》 。我們每日會就 水質進行檢測，每月會記錄化學需氧量 （ 「 COD 」 ） 、生物需氧量 （ 「 BOD 」 ） 、酸鹼值、 懸浮固體 （ 「 SS 」 ） 、氨，以及污泥的含水 量、有機物和酸鹼值等數。我們亦會進 行定期檢測，確保污水於排放前的水質， 以期推動環境保護及可持續發展。

## 生物燃料顆粒生產

強泰環保的⼦公司 RPSL 在印尼佔碑主要 營運生物燃料顆粒生產業務。主要生產 原材料為木製品生產過程所產生的木質 顆粒。我們致⼒提升木質顆粒利⽤率， 以減少廢棄物產生，從而減低對環境的 負擔。我們現時已完成建設生產設施， 以處理作為原材料的木質顆粒。設施於 ⼆零⼆⼆年中開始營運，並向主要目標 顧客，括東南亞的發電廠銷售經處理 的木質顆粒。

為保障生物質發電及生物質燃料粒生

## BOARD STATEMENT AND GOVERNANCE STRUCTURE

## BOARD'S OVERSIGHT OF ESG ISSUES

The  Board  is  responsible  for  overseeing  the  management  of environmental,  social,  and  governance  risks,  while  considering stakeholders'  concerns  to  determine  material  issues  for  the Group.  The  Group  has  implemented  risk  management  and internal  control  mechanisms  to  identify,  manage,  and  monitor risks,  including  ESG-related  risks.  We  regularly  review  and refine  these  mechanisms.  For  further  information  regarding  the Group's  corporate  governance  measures,  please  refer  to  the Corporate Governance Report in this Annual Report.

## ESG MANAGEMENT APPROACH AND STRATEGY

The  Group  has  established  dedicated  teams  within  each business  division  to  manage  ESG  issues.  They  are  responsible for  overseeing  the  implementation  of  the  Group's  sustainability strategy, updating sustainability policies, reviewing sustainability risks, monitoring sustainability performance, goals, and targets, and reviewing feedback from stakeholders. They report on these sustainability matters regularly to the Board for decision-making purposes.

## ESG-RELATED GOALS AND TARGETS

We are committed to integrating sustainability practices into our daily  operations  and  aligning  our  sustainability  goals  with  our strategic  direction,  creating  innovative  and  productive  spaces that  generate  sustainable  values  for  our  stakeholders.  We continue  to  monitor  and  oversee  progress  towards  corporate goals  and  targets,  address  climate  change,  and  improve  our performance on key ESG matters.

產業務品質，我們已制定標準營運程序 （ 「 SOP 」 ） ，指導員工於日常營運維持嚴格 標準。

## 董事會聲明和管治架構

## 董事會對 ESG 問題的監督

董事會負責監督集團整體的環境、社會 及管治風險，並考慮持份者所關注的議 題。本集團已建立風險管理及內部管控 機制，識別、管理和監測風險，括環 境、社會及管治相關風險。我們定期審 視及完善這些機制。有關本集團在企業 管治措施的更多細節，請參考本年度報 告中的企業管治報告。

## ESG 管理方法和戰略

我們於各業務板塊建立團隊，以管理 ESG 相關事宜。這些團隊會負責監督集 團整體可持續發展策略的實施、更新的 可持續發展政策、審視可持續發展風險、 監督持份者的意見，同時向董事會定期 匯報，以供決策之⽤。

## 與 ESG 相關的目標和指標

我們致⼒於將可持續發展常規融入日常 營運，確保可持續發展目標與戰略方向 保持⼀致，為持份者締造創新及具生產 ⼒的空間，創造持久價值。我們持續監 測和監督企業目標和指標的進展，應對 氣候變化，改善我們於重點 ESG 事宜的 表現。

報告原則

| Principles  Definitions  Responses from the Group  原則  定義  集團回應  Materiality  The threshold at which ESG issues  determined by the board are sufficiently  important to investors and other  stakeholders that they should be reported.  Through engaging with stakeholders as well  as considering the Group's business nature  and development, material sustainability  issues are identified.  重要性  當董事會釐定有關  ESG  事宜會對投資者及  其他持份者產生重要影響時，發行人就應  作出匯報。  通過與利益相關者的接觸以及考慮集團的  業務性質和發展，確定重大的可持續發展  問題。  Quantitative  KPIs in respect of historical data need to be  measurable. The issuer should set targets  (which may be actual numerical figures or  directional, forward-looking statements)  to reduce a particular impact. In this way  the effectiveness of ESG policies and  management systems can be evaluated  and validated. Quantitative information  should be accompanied by a narrative,  explaining its purpose, impacts, and giving  comparative data where appropriate.  The Group discloses its key environmental  and social performance indicators  quantitatively where appropriate.  量化  有關歷史數據的關鍵績效指標須可予計量。  發行人應訂下減少個別影響的目標 （可能為  實際數字或方向性、前瞻性的聲明） ，使環  境、社會及管治政策及管理系統的效益可  被評估及驗證。量化數據應附帶說明，闡  述其目的及影響，並在適當的情況下提供  比較數據。  集團在適當的時候以量化的方式披露其關  鍵的環境和社會績效指標。  Balance  The ESG report should provide an unbiased  picture of the issuer's performance.  The report should avoid selections,  omissions, or presentation formats that  may inappropriately influence a decision or  judgment by the report reader.  The Group has identified and disclosed  the environmental, social and governance  issues with significant impact on the  Group's business, including the results  and challenges faced by the Group, in this  Report.  平衡  環境、社會及管治報告應當不偏不倚地呈  現，避免可能會不恰當地影響報告讀者決  策或判斷的選擇、遺漏或呈報格式。  集團已在本報告中確定並披露了對集團業  務有重大影響的環境、社會及管治問題，  括集團的成果和面臨的挑戰。  Consistency  The issuer should use consistent  methodologies to allow for meaningful  comparisons of ESG data over time.  The reporting scope and reporting method  are substantially consistent with those of  the prior year(s), and this Report has also  disclosed relevant comparative information.  ⼀致性  發行人應使⽤⼀致的披露統計方法，令環  境、社會及管治數據日後可作有意義的比  較。  報告範圍和報告方法與上⼀年度基本⼀致，  本報告也披露了相關的比較資訊。   |

## STAKEHOLDER ENGAGEMENT

The  Group  places  a  strong  emphasis  on  stakeholder communication  and  actively  seeks  to  incorporate  appropriate opinions  and  suggestions  into  our  continuous  improvement process.  We  have  identified  contractors,  shareholders and  investors,  government  authorities,  non-governmental organizations  (NGOs),  environmental  protection  departments (EPDs),  suppliers,  business  partners,  and  the  neighboring community as our key stakeholder groups. We collect feedback from  these  groups  regarding  the  Group's  operations  and sustainable  development  performance  through  various  formal and informal two-way communication channels.

## 持份者參與

本集團重視持份者溝通，積極採納適 當的意見及建議，以持續改善。我們已 識別定承商、股東和投資者、政府 當局、⾮政府組織 ( 「 NGO 」 ) 、環保部門 ( 「 EPD 」 ) 、供應商、商業夥伴和鄰近社區 為我們的主要持份者組別。我們透過不 同形式及⾮正式雙向溝通渠道，收集這 些組別對本集團營運及可持續發展表現 的反饋。

## MATERIALITY ASSESSMENT

In  addition  to  regular  stakeholder  communications  as  outlined above,  we  engage  an  independent  sustainability  consultant to  conduct  a  materiality  assessment  on  a  yearly  basis.  This assessment  involves  collecting  stakeholders'  views  through  an online  survey  to  identify  the  materiality  of  various  sustainability issues. We believe that the results of the materiality assessment can  help  us  identify  environmental,  social,  and  governance issues  that  are  material  to  the  Group,  allowing  us  to  improve our sustainability approach and strategies more effectively. The process of the materiality assessment covers:

1. Identify  27  ESG  issues  that  are  of  concern  to  the  Group based on recent and available stakeholder communication findings, listing requirements and latest sustainability trend in the industry.
2. Invite  stakeholders  from  various  sectors  to  fill  in  an  online survey by rating the materiality of the issues to the Group.
3. Carry out materiality  analysis  based  on  the  collected  data and combine with the assessment of the management.

## 重要性評估

除了上述與的定期溝通，我們亦委託獨 立可持續發展顧問每年進行重要性評估。 評估通過線上調查收集持份者意見，以 識別不同可持續發展議題的重要性。我 們相信，重要性評估的結果可以幫助我 們識別對集團有重大影響的環境、社會 及管治議題，讓我們改善可持續發展方 法及策略。重要性評估的過程涵蓋：

1. 根據近期和適⽤的持份者溝通結果、 上市要求和最新可持續發展行業趨 勢，識別本集團關注的 27 個 ESG 議 題。
2. 邀請來自不同組別的持份者填寫網 上問卷，就議題對本集團的重要性 進行評分。
3. 根據所收集數據進行重要性分析， 並與管理層的評估合併。

## OUR APPROACH IN SUSTAINABILITY

As  a  leading  environmental  solutions  provider  in  the  PRC and  Indonesia,  ELL  Environmental  recognizes  its  role  as  a responsible  corporate  citizen  and  is  committed  to  pursuing sustainable  operations.  To  this  end,  we  have  developed  the Environmental, Social, and Governance Policy, along with other related  policies  and  measures,  to  guide  each  department  and employee  in  incorporating  sustainable  principles  into  daily operations.  These  principles  include  protecting  employees' rights,  ensuring  occupational  health  and  safety,  promoting product  and  service  responsibility,  combating  corruption, and  prioritizing  environmental  protection  at  all  levels  of  our operations. Our ultimate goal is to create a better, greener, and more equitable society for future generations. The following four sections  in  this  Report  outline  our  key  strategies,  policies,  and achievements in sustainable development:

## Corporate Governance

企業管治

Assess, prevent and manage environmental, social and governance risks and maintain high-level business ethics and integrity

評估、防止和管理環境、社 會及管治風險，並維持高度 商業道德和誠信

## Caring Our People

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## CREATING GREEN FORTUNE

The  Group  considers  its  fundamental  responsibility  to  bring positive  impact  to  the  environment.  Therefore,  we  have i ntroduced  comprehensive  policies  and  measures  in  our daily  operations,  and  at  the  same  time  we  strictly  adhere  to applicable  laws  and  regulations  in  the  jurisdictions  where  we operate  to  regulate  our  environmental  performance,  as  well as  matters  relating  to  health  and  safety,  advertising,  labeling, and  privacy  concerning  our  environmental  solutions  business. These  laws  and  regulations  include,  but  are  not  limited  to,  the Environmental  Protection  Law  of  the  PRC,  the  Atmospheric Pollution  Prevention  and  Control  Law  of  the  PRC,  the  Water Pollution Prevention and Control Law of the PRC, the Law of the PRC  on  the  Prevention  and  Control  of  Environmental  Pollution by  Solid  Waste,  Law  No.  32/2009  on  Environmental  Protection and  Management,  Government  Regulation  No.  41/1999  on  Air Pollution  Control,  Government  Regulation  No.  101/2014  on Management  of  Hazardous  Waste  and  Toxic,  Law  No.  7/2004 on  Water  Resources,  the  Regulation  of  the  Minister  of  Energy and Mineral Resources No. 12/2017 on Utilization of Renewable Energy Sources for Power Supply of the Republic of Indonesia, as well as the Advertising Law of the PRC.

During  the  Reporting  Period,  the  Group  was  not  aware  of any  material  non-compliance  concerning  relevant  laws  and regulations.

## 創造綠色財富

本集團視為環境帶來正面影響為我們的 重任。因此，我們於日常營運實踐全方 位政策措施，謹遵營運當地適⽤的法律 及規例以規範我們的環境表現，以及與 環保方案業務有關的健康與安全、廣告 及標籤及私隱相關的事宜。這些法律及 規例括但不限於《中華人民共和國環境 保護法》 、 《中華人民共和國大氣污染防 治法》 、 《中華人民共和國水污染防治法》 、 《中華人民共和國固體廢物污染環境防治 法》 、第 32/2009 號 《環境保護和管理法》 、 有關空氣污染控制的第 41/1999 號、政府 法規、有關有害及有毒物質廢棄物、管 理的第 101/2014 號政府法規、有關水資源 的第 7/2004 號法律、有關利⽤可再生能 源以便於印度尼西亞供電的第 12/2017 號 能源及礦產資源部部長令，以及 《中華人 民共和國廣告法》 。

於報告期間，本集團並不知悉任何重大 的相關法律及規例的違規情況。

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## 廢氣、溫室氣體排放和能源消耗 AIR, GREENHOUSE GAS EMISSIONS AND USE OF ENERGY

The  Group's  air  emissions,  greenhouse  gas  (GHG)  emissions, and  energy  consumption  are  primarily  attributed  to  purchased electricity,  fuel  consumption  of  company  vehicles,  machinery, and  other  equipment,  as  well  as  air  pollutants  and  odors generated  during  operations.  As  an  environmental  solutions service  provider,  we  acknowledge  our  responsibility  to  reduce our  air  and  GHG  emissions  and  optimize  the  use  of  energy  to combat global warming and climate change through a variety of measures:

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Managing air emissions 管理廢氣排放

Minimizing odor 減少異味

Optimizing use of energy 優化能源消耗

本集團的廢氣、溫室氣體排放及能源消 耗主要源自外購電⼒、公司車輛、機器 及其他設施的燃油消耗，以及因營運而 產生的廢氣和臭氣。作為環保方案提供 商，我們有責任減少自身的廢氣及溫室 氣體排放，並優化能源使⽤，透過以下 措施，應對溫室效應及氣候變化問題：

- Build atmospheric environmental protection zone surrounding exterior to our wastewater treatment facilities to minimize the negative impact of air pollutants imposed to the surrounding environment
- 在污水處理設施周邊建設大氣環境保護區，盡量減少空 氣污染物對周邊居民的負面影響
- Conduct regular testing on air pollutants to ensure the emissions meet industrial and local standards
- 進行定期檢測，確保廢氣排放符合行業及本地標準
- Build deodorization facilities in the wastewater treatment facilities to absorb odor
- 在污水處理設施建設除臭設施
- Adopt biological deodorization technology in Rugao Hengfa Facilities
- 在如皋恆發設施採取生物除臭技術
- Plant trees around the facilities to absorb odor
- 在設施周圍種植樹木，以吸收氣味
- Collect and transport waste properly to reduce odor
- 妥善儲存和運輸廢物，以減少異味
- Turn off the engines of idled vehicles
- 汽車閒置時關閉引擎
- Require employees to turn off electrical appliances while leaving
- 要求員工離開時關掉⽤電設備
- Turn down the air flowrate of blowers according to the quality and level of dissolved oxygen of incoming wastewater in the wastewater treatment facilities to reduce use of energy
- 根據入廠污水水質和溶解氧氣調低鼓風機風量，減低能 源消耗

<!-- image -->

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## WASTE EMISSIONS

We  recognize  that  our  business  activities  generate  both non-hazardous  and  hazardous  waste,  including  sludge,  waste oil, used batteries, general refuse, chemicals, jerry cans, and oil filters. We acknowledge that these waste emissions can burden the environment. As such, we are committed to managing them in a stringent and responsible manner by promoting the concept of  reducing  waste  at  the  source  and  handling  waste  emissions properly. We have established a waste management system that guides employees to separate different types of non-recyclable waste, recyclable waste, and hazardous waste. Our wastewater plants  have  hazardous  waste  storage  facilities  in  place  to temporarily  store  hazardous  waste  in  an  appropriate  manner and  store  chemicals  safely  to  prevent  spills.  We  have  engaged sanitation  service  providers  to  collect  general  refuse  and screener  residue  and  third-party  providers  with  the  necessary qualifications  to  collect  and  handle  dewatered  sewage  sludge generated from wastewater facilities.

Furthermore,  we  highly  promote  the  concept  of  'green  office' to  minimize  the  use  of  office  paper  and  reduce  waste  at  the source.  We  encourage  our  employees  to  adopt  electronic communication,  double-sided  printing,  and  reuse  single-sided paper.

## WASTEWATER EMISSIONS AND USE OF WATER

ELL  Environmental  recognizes  the  importance  of  water conservation  and  the  responsible  management  of  water r e s o u r c e s .   The  Group  consumes  both  municipal  and underground  water  to  support  its  daily  operations  but  has not  encountered  any  issues  with  water  sourcing.  To  conserve water  resources,  the  Group  advocates  for  the  reuse  of  treated wastewater,  such  as  for  cleaning  equipment.  We  also  generate wastewater  during  its  operations.  We  have  established wastewater  treatment  facilities  at  Rugao  Hengfa  to  treat industrial  wastewater  before  discharge.  In  addition,  we  have built  a  wastewater  treatment  facility  at  RPSL  to  treat  industrial wastewater  and  septic  tanks  to  treat  municipal  wastewater before  discharge.  We  conduct  regular  inspections  of  water quality  to  ensure  it  meets  the  requirements  of  Government Regulation  No.  82/2001  on  the  Management  of  Water  Quality, with  inspection  criteria  including  total  suspended  solids  (TSS), pH, metals, and phosphate.

## 廢物排放

我們的業務會產生無害及有害廢棄物， 括污泥、廢油脂、廢棄電池、⼀般廢 棄物、化學物質、油罐及油濾器。我們 意識到廢棄物排放會對環境造成負擔。 故此，我們致⼒以嚴謹負責任的態度妥 善處理廢棄物，並從源頭減廢。我們已 建立廢棄物管理制度，指導員工將不同 類別的⾮回收、可回收及有害廢棄物分 類收集。我們的污水廠設有有害廢棄物 倉庫，以妥善暫存有害廢棄物及化學品， 以防止洩漏。我們已委託排污服務商收 集⼀般廢棄物和篩渣，並委託合資格的 第三方收集及處理由污水處理設施產生 的脫水污泥。

此外，我們大⼒提倡 「綠色辦公」 的理念， 從源頭減少⽤紙，降低廢棄物產生量。 我們鼓勵員工採⽤電⼦化溝通、雙面影 印，並重⽤單面印刷紙張。

## 廢水排放及⽤水

強泰環保重視節約⽤水及負責任的⽤水 管理。本集團會消耗生活⽤水和地下水， 以⽀持日常營運。我們在求取適⽤水源 上並無遇到任何問題。為節省⽤水，本 集團會回⽤經處理後的污水作清潔之⽤。 我們亦會在營運當中產生污水。我們已 於如皋恆發設置污水處理設施，將污水 進行預處理，方可對外排放。我們亦於 RPSL 建設污水處理設施，處理工業污水， 並設有化糞池，處理城鎮污水，方可對 外排放。我們會定期檢測水質，確保其 合乎第 82/2001 號關於水質管理的政府規 例。檢測範疇括總懸浮固體，酸鹼值， 金屬及磷酸鹽等。

## NOISE EMISSIONS

The Group understands that its daily operations, particularly  at its plants, may generate noise that could have negative impacts on the surrounding environment. To minimize these effects, we have implemented the following measures:

- Purchase low-noise equipment if possible
- Install  vibration  absorber  and  damping  base  to  reduce noise generated from equipment
- Install  rubber  damper  on  windows  and  doors  to  absorb noise
- Plant  trees  or  bamboos  surrounding  the  plants  to  absorb noise
- Conduct  regular  inspection  to  ensure  equipment  are  in good conditions without noise from friction or loose parts

## MANAGING IMPACT ON ENVIRONMENT AND NATURAL RESOURCES

The Group is committed to identifying and managing the impact of  its  operations  on  the  environment  and  natural  resources  to minimize  the  company's  carbon  footprint  and  promote  positive environmental outcomes.

For  RPSL  in  Indonesia,  we  have  conducted  an  environmental i mpact  assessment  to  identify  and  assess  the  potential environmental  impacts  of  the  construction  of  its  biofuel  pellet production  plant.  The  assessment  report  identified  emissions, including  waste,  air  emissions,  and  wastewater,  and  outlined measures  to  properly  handle  and  reduce  such  emissions  to minimize  the  impact  on  the  environment.  ELL  Environmental  is dedicated  to  promoting  sustainable  practices  and  minimizing the environmental impact of its operations.

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## 噪音排放

本集團明白日常營運，尤其廠區會產生噪 音，會對周邊環境造成負面影響。為減 少這些影響，我們已實施以下措施：

- 在可行情況下選購低噪音設備
- 安裝減震底座和避震器，減少設備 所產生的噪音
- 在門窗上安裝橡膠減震器以吸收噪 音
- 在廠區外圍種植樹木或竹⼦以吸收 噪音
- 進行定期檢測，確保設備狀況良好， 不會因磨擦或零件鬆動而產生噪音

## 管理對環境和自然資源的影響

本集團致⼒識別及管理營運對環境及天 然資源所造成的影響，減少集團碳足跡， 為環境創造正面影響。

在印尼 RPSL ，我們已進行環境影響評估， 識別及評估建設生物質燃料粒生產設施 時的潛在環境影響。評估報告列明相關 排放，括廢棄物、廢氣及污水排放， 以及相關應對措施，以妥善處理及減少 該等排放，務求減少對環境的負擔。強 泰環保致⼒提倡可持續發展常規，減少 營運產生的環境影響。

## CLIMATE RESILIENCE STRATEGIES

In  view  of  the  worsening  climate  change  problem,  we  strive  to take  up  our  responsibility  to  address  climate  change  issues. The  Group  has  integrated  physical,  chronic,  and  transitional risks  related  to  climate  change  into  its  risk  assessments  and strategies.  We  have  referenced  the  Financial  Stability  Board's Task  Force  on  Climate-Related  Financial  Disclosures  (TCFD) framework  to  manage  climate-related  risks  and  opportunities. The  climate-related  risks  and  opportunities  identified  for  the company include:

## Risks 風險

Acute physical risk

Extreme weather event such as a super typhoon or other causing flooding, damage to buildings and facilities, resulting in asset impairment and increased maintenance costs.

急性實體風險

極端天氣事件，如超強颱風或其他造成洪水、建築和設施損壞，導致資產減值和 維修費⽤增加。

Chronic physical risk

Long term change in climate such as a rainfall patterns causing repeated flooding which affect the effectiveness of normal operation and speed up the replacement need of equipment

慢性實體風險

氣候的長期變化，如降雨模式造成反覆的水災，影響正常運行的效果，加速設備 的更換需求。

Transitional risks

Increase in ESG related policies and legal requirements increase our cost of operation.

過渡性風險

ESG 相關政策和法律要求的增加加重了我們的營運成本。

## Opportunities

機遇

Sustainable cities

Increase in market demand of wastewater treatment and new energy enhance the number of available projects in the market that allow us to participate in and grow our business.

可持續社區

污水處理和新能源市場需求的增加提高了市場上可⽤的項目數量，使我們能夠參 與並發展我們的業務。

We  acknowledge  the  importance  of  addressing  the opportunities  and  risks  of  climate  change  and  have  developed the Environmental, Social, and Governance Management Policy that  includes  a  climate  change  strategy.  The  policy  aims  to strengthen our climate risk management and performance. ELL Environmental  has  set  targets  to  reduce  the  intensity  of  scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions to mitigate its impact on climate change.

我們意識到應對氣候變化風險及機遇的 重要性，並制定環境、社會及管治政策， 當中涵蓋氣候變化戰略。政策旨在提升 氣候變化管理及表現。我們已設定減少 溫室氣體排放 （範圍 1 及 2 ） 密度的目標，減 少對氣候變化的影響。

## 氣候韌性策略

鑑於氣候變化問題越趨嚴重，我們致⼒ 肩負應對氣候變化事宜的責任。本集團 已在風險評估和戰略中納入了與氣候變 化風險有關的急性實體、慢性實體和過 渡性風險。我們參照金融穩定委員會的 氣候相關財務信息披露工作組 ( TCFD) 的 框架管理氣候變化相關風險及機遇。本 集團識別的氣候變化相關風險及機遇 括：

## CARING OUR PEOPLE

ELL  Environmental  values  each  employee's  contribution  to  the company's  sustainable  development  and  strives  to  provide  a healthy  and  safe  workplace  with  a  supportive  atmosphere.  The company  adheres  strictly  to  labor  laws,  including  the  'Labour Law  of  the  PRC,'  the  'Labour  Contract  Law  of  the  PRC,' 'Provisions  on  the  Prohibition  of  Using  Child  Labour,'  and  the Government  Regulation  No.  50/2012  on  the  Implementation  of Occupational  Safety  and  Health  Management  Systems  of  the Republic  of  Indonesia.  We  have  a  number  of  policies  in  place that  cover  terms  of  compensation,  dismissal,  recruitment, promotion,  working  hours,  rest  periods,  equal  opportunity, diversity,  antidiscrimination,  benefits,  and  welfare  to  safeguard the interests of our employees.

During the Reporting Period, ELL Environmental was not aware of any significant violations of relevant laws and regulations.

## 關愛員工

強泰環保重視每位員工對集團可持續發 展進程的貢獻，致⼒提供⼀個健康、安 全的職場和融合的工作氛圍。我們謹遵 僱傭相關法律及規例，括《中華人民共 和國勞動法》 、 《中華人民共和國勞動合同 法》 、 《禁止使⽤童工規定》 及關於印度尼 西亞共和國實施職業安全健康管理制度 第 50/2012 號政府法規。我們制定⼀系列 有關補償及解僱、招聘及晉升、工作時 數、休息時間、平等機會、多元化、反 歧視及其他福利的政策，保障員工利益。

於報告期間，本集團並不知悉任何重大 違反相關法律及規例的情況。

We  have formulated rigorous occupational health and safety measures to protect its employees, including:

## 員工安康

我們重視員工安康。本集團謹遵相關法 律及規例以確保員工的健康與安全， 括但不限於《中華人民共和國安全生產法》 及關於印度尼西亞共和國實施職業安全 健康管理制度第 50/2012 號政府法規。我 們已制定嚴謹的職業健康與安全措施保 障員工，括：

- Providing  Personal  Protective  Equipment  (' PPE ')  to  applicable  employees including  gloves,  work  clothes,  masks,  ear  plug,  ear  muff,  safety  helmet  and welding glasses
- 為適⽤員工提供個人保護設備，括手套、工作服、面罩、耳塞、安全帽及 電焊保護鏡
- Conducting inspections on a regular basis on fire-fighting equipment, facilities and electrical appliances
- 就防火設備、設施和電器進行定期檢查
- Conducting  fire  and  emergency  drills  regularly  as  well  as  arranging  safety trainings to ensure employees are equipped with relevant knowledge and skills
- 定期進行火警及應急演習，並為員工安排安全培訓，確保他們具備相關知識 及技能
- Arranging designated personnel to oversee the safety of the work area
- 安排指定人員負責工作地點安全
- Organizing sport and leisure activities for employees to relax
- 舉辦體育及休閒活動，讓員工放鬆身心

During  the  past  three  years  including  the  Reporting  Period, the  Group  was  not  aware  of  any  work-related  fatalities.  there were  four  minor  work-related  injuries  in  Indonesia  during  the Reporting Period and resulted in 34 man-day lost.

在過去三年 （括本次報告期間） ，集團沒 有發現任何與工作相關的死亡事件。另 外，於報告期間，印尼有四宗與工作相 關的輕度工傷事件，導致 34 日因工傷損 失工作日數。

## EMPLOYEE DEVELOPMENT

We  recognize  the  importance  of  providing  employees with  training  and  promotion  opportunities  to  support  their professional growth alongside the Group's development.

We  strive  to  establish  and  maintain  a  comprehensive  career development  ladder  that  offers  equal  opportunities  for promotion  based  on  performance  appraisal  results.  Regular performance  appraisals  are  conducted  to  evaluate  employees' work performance, abilities, and skills.

In  terms  of  training,  we  offer  a  variety  of  training  programs  to enable employees to acquire professional knowledge and skills and  strive  for  excellence.  During  the  Reporting  Period,  we offered  over  420  hours  of  training  to  our  employees. ## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## LABOR STANDARDS

We  are  committed  to  being  a  responsible  and  desirable employer,  and  strictly  adhere  to  applicable  labor  standards  in all  jurisdictions  where  it  operates  and  prohibits  the  recruitment of  child  and  forced  labor.  During  the  recruitment  process,  we will  verify  the  personal  identity  documents  of  candidates  to ensure  they  have  met  the  legal  working  age  to  prevent  the occurrence  of  child  labor.  We  have  also  established  clear policies  on  rest  periods,  leave,  overtime  work,  working  hours, dismissal,  and  other  related  arrangements  to  prevent  forced labor.  In  cases  of  staff  resignation  or  dismissal,  we  will  offer appropriate  compensation  in  accordance  with  applicable  labor laws and regulations. Any instances of illegal or forced labor are investigated  and  addressed  in  compliance  with  local  laws  and regulations.

During  the  Reporting  Period,  we  were  not  aware  of  any instances of child or forced labor within the Group.

## MANAGING SUPPLIERS

As  at  31  December  2022,  we  have  engaged  678  suppliers  in which 621 are in Indonesia, 1 in Singapore and 56 in China. We mainly  worked  with  local  suppliers  to  minimize  the  greenhouse gas emissions generated from transportation.

We are acknowledged to manage the environmental and social impacts of suppliers and promote positive outcomes throughout the entire supply chain. We have already established a Code of Conduct that requires suppliers to uphold environmental, social, and  governance  principles,  including  environmental  protection, labor  rights,  labor  standards,  and  anti-corruption  practices when providing products and services. We will conduct regular assessments  to  ensure  that  suppliers  comply  with  the  Code of  Conduct.  If  any  suppliers  are  found  to  be  noncompliant,  we may  terminate  the  partnership  or  require  corrective  measures. During the Reporting Period, we have conducted a review of 51 suppliers located in Mainland China.

## 勞工準則

我們致⼒成為負責任和理想僱主，嚴格 遵守營運當地適⽤的僱傭標準，嚴禁聘 請童工及強制勞工。在招聘過程中，我 們會核實應聘者的身份證明文件，確保 彼等已達到法定工作年齡，避免童工。 我們亦制定政策，清晰列明有關作息時 間、假期、加班、工作時數、解僱等的安 排，避免強制勞動。如有員工辭職或被 解僱，我們會根據適⽤僱傭法律及規例 提供適當補償。如有發現任何僱⽤⾮法 或強迫勞動的情況，我們會根據當地政 府和當局制定的相關法律和法規進行調 查和處理。

在報告期間，我們並不知悉本集團內有 任何童工或強制勞動的情況。

## 管理供應商

截止 2022 年 12 月 31 日，本集團共與 678 家 供應商合作，其中 621 間位於印尼、 1 間 位於新加坡、 56 間位於中國大陸。我們 主要與本地供應商合作，以減少因運輸 產生的溫室氣體排放。

我們重視管理供應商的環境及社會影響， 為整體供應鏈帶來正面影響。我們已制 定行為守則，要求供應商提供產品及服 務時秉持環境、社會及管治原則，括 環境保護、僱傭權益、僱傭標準及反貪 污常規。我們會進行定期評估，確保彼 等符合行為守則。如發現有任何不合規 情況，我們或會終止合作關係，或要求 供應商採取整改措施。於報告期間，我 們對中國其中 51 家供應商進行了檢討評 估。

In  addition,  we  also  place  significant  importance  on  the  quality of  suppliers'  products  and  services,  as  they  have  a  direct i mpact  on  the  quality  of  the  our  operations.  We  conduct rigorous  assessments  of  potential  new  suppliers,  evaluating their  qualifications,  product  and  service  quality,  price,  and reputation  to  ensure  they  meet  our  requirements.  Similarly, existing  suppliers  are  also  regularly  assessed  to  ensure  they have  met  our  expectations.  We  are  dedicated  to  maintaining high standards of product and service quality across its supply chain to deliver superior value to its customers.

## PRODUCT RESPONSIBILITY

## PRIVACY PROTECTION

Our  business  operations  involve  handling  confidential i n f o r m a t i o n ,   i n c l u d i n g   customer  information,  business information,  and  financial  information  of  the  Group.  To  ensure the  privacy  of  this  information  and  to  alleviate  customer concerns,  we  have  implemented  measures  to  handle  it  with care.  These  measures  include  requiring  employees  to  abide  by our  personnel  management  policy  and  employee  handbook, and  not  disclosing  privacy  information  to  third  parties  without prior consent. We also use passwords for computers that store confidential  information,  and  store  hard  copies  of  sensitive materials  in  a  designated  place  with  an  electronic  lock  and 24-hour surveillance cameras.

## ADVERTISING

The  Group  strictly  adheres  to  the  Advertising  Law  of  the PRC  and  other  relevant  laws  and  regulations  when  creating promotional  materials.  We  conduct  a  thorough  review  of  these materials before publishing to ensure that there is no misleading information  that  may  harm  consumer  rights.  As  our  business does not heavily rely on product labelling, this is not a significant issue for the Group.

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report

環境、社會及管治報告

此外，我們十分重視對業務有重大影響 的供應商產品及服務質素。我們對候選 新供應商處很嚴格評估，評估其資質、 產品及服務質素、價錢及聲譽等，確保 他們符合集團要求。同樣地，我們會對 現有供應商進行定期評估，確保彼等符 合我們的期望。我們致⼒於供應鏈維持 高品質的產品及服務質素，以期為客戶 締造價值。

## 產品責任

## 隱私保護

本集團的業務營運會處理機密資料， 括客戶資料、集團的業務資料及財務資 料。為保護資料私隱，確保客戶安心， 我們已實施謹慎措施，括要求員工遵 守人⼒資源相關制度及員工手冊， ' 在未 經同意前不得將隱私資料對第三方披露。 我們亦會於儲存機密資料的電腦設置密 碼，並將印有敏感資料的文件儲存於具 備電⼦鎖和 24 小時監控攝像鏡頭的地方。

## 廣告

本集團致⼒遵守《中華人民共和國廣告法》 及其他適⽤法律及規例制定宣傳材料。 我們在發佈這些材料前會進行全面評估， 確保沒有具誤導性的資訊，以免損害消 費者權益。由於產品標籤並⾮集團業務 的重要範疇，因此標籤並⾮集團重大議 題。

## Environmental, Social and Governance Report 環境、社會及管治報告

## ANTI-CORRUPTION

## UPHOLDING BUSINESS ETHICS AND INTEGRITY

The  Group  is  committed  to  maintaining  a  fair  and  transparent business  environment  and  promoting  ethical  behavior  in all  our  operations.  We  strictly  adhere  to  the  'Law  of  the PRC  on  Anti-Unfair  Competition',  the  'Criminal  Law  of  the PRC',  'Interim  Provisions  on  Banning  Commercial  Bribery', Hong  Kong's  'Prevention  of  Bribery  Ordinance'  and  other relevant  laws  and  regulations,  prohibiting  corruption,  bribery, extortion,  fraud,  money  laundering  and  other  unethical  and unlawful  behaviours  within  the  Group.  We  have  introduced the  Anti-corruption  Policy  and  Code  of  Conduct,  requiring  our employees to maintain business ethics and integrity at all times. We  have  also  established  the  Whistleblowing  Policy,  allowing employees  and  third  parties  to  report  suspicious  cases  or other misconduct while keeping their identities confidential. We conduct prompt investigations and take corresponding follow-up actions  seriously  to  protect  the  rights  of  the  Group  and  our stakeholders.

We will  arrange  trainings  from  time  to  time  to  raise  employees' awareness of relevant issues. During the Reporting Period, due to  spread  of  pandemic,  we  have  not  arranged  anti-corruption related  trainings  to  our  employees.  We  will  actively  seek appropriate  opportunities  to  arrange  relevant  trainings  in  the coming year.

In  the  Reporting  Period,  the  Group  was  not  aware  of  any material  non-compliance  relating  to  corruption,  bribery, extortion, fraud and money laundering.

## CONTRIBUTING TO COMMUNITY

ELL  Environmental  is  committed  to  listening  to  and  addressing the needs of the community. We believe that striking a balance between  business  development  and  community  investment  is a  core  principle  of  our  sustainable  strategy.  We  are  dedicated to  wastewater  treatment  and  participating  in  new  energy, which  benefits  the  community  as  a  whole.  We  allocate adequate  resources  each  year  to  community  investment  and encourage our employees to volunteer to showcase our efforts in  taking  up  corporate  social  responsibility.  We  believe  that community investment is an important aspect of our sustainable development and we will continue to evaluate and improve our approach to ensure that it aligns with our values and goals.

## 反腐倡廉

## 維護商業道德和誠信

本集團致⼒維護公平、透明的營商環境， 於業務提倡道德行為。我們致⼒遵守《中 華人民共和國反不正當競爭法》 、 《中華人 民共和國刑法》 ） 、 《關於禁止商業賄賂行 為的暫行規定》 ） 、香港防止賄賂條例及 其他相關法律及規例，禁止集團出現任 何貪污、賄賂、勒索、欺詐、洗黑錢及 其他不道德及⾮法行為。我們已實施反 貪污政策和行為守則，要求員工時刻秉 持商業道德及誠信。我們亦制定了舉報 政策，讓員工及第三方就懷疑涉貪個案 或其他違規情況作出舉報。我們會確保 彼等身份保密。我們會及時嚴謹地作出 調查及跟進行動，保障集團及持份者的 利益。

我們會適時安排培訓，提供員工對相關 議題的意識。於報告期間，基於疫情肆 虐，我們並未為員工安排反貪污相關培 訓。我們會積極尋求合適機會，於來年 度安排相關培訓。

於報告期間，本集團並不知悉在貪污、 賄賂、勒索、欺詐及洗黑錢方面涉及重 大違規情況。

## 社區貢獻

強泰環保致⼒聆聽及回應社區需要。我 們相關在業務發展和社區投資取得平衡 是集團可持續發展策略的重要⼀環。我 們竭⼒投身於污水處理及新能源業務， 為社區帶來裨益。我們每年均會投放足 夠資源於社區投資，鼓勵員工擔任義工， 以彰顯我們實踐企業社會責任的決心。 我們相關社區投資是可持續發展的重要 ⼀環，會持續檢討及優化我們的策略， 確保符合我們的價值及目標。

During the Reporting Period, RPSL has donated RP54,025,955 to  support  local  residents  in  need,  as  well  as  celebration of  festivals  in  the  surrounding  community.  It  also  provided internship  opportunities  to  students,  allowing  them  to  equip themselves with adequate job skills.

## EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS

## Mr. Chau On Ta Yuen

Mr.  Chau  On  Ta  Yuen,  aged  75,  was  appointed  as  a  Director of  the  Company  on  18  March  2014  and  was  re-designated  as the  chairman  of  the  Board  (the  ' Chairman ')  and  an  executive Director  of  the  Company  on  5  September  2014.  He  has  been the  chairman  of  the  nomination  committee  of  the  Board  (the ' Nomination  Committee ')  since  5  September  2014.  Mr. Chau  also  holds  directorships  in  certain  subsidiaries  of  the Company  and  is  mainly  responsible  for  business  development and formulation of strategies of the Group. He joined the Group in  December  2002  as  a  director  of  Everbest  Water  Treatment Development Company Limited (' Everbest '), an indirect whollyowned subsidiary of the Company. Mr. Chau is a director of and owns 90% of the issued shares in Wealthy Sea Holdings Limited (' Wealthy Sea '), a substantial shareholder of the Company.

Mr.  Chau  obtained  a  bachelor's  degree  in  Chinese  language and literature from Xiamen University ( 廈門大學 )  in the People's Republic of China (the ' PRC ') in August 1968. He is currently a member of the 12th National Committee of the Chinese People's Political  Consultative  Conference  ( 中國人民政治協商會議 ),  a deputy officer of the Social and Legal Affairs Committee of the Chinese  People's  Political  Consultative  Conference  ( 全國政協 社會和法制委員會 )  and  the  vice  chairman  and  the  secretarygeneral  of  the  10th  board  of  directors  of  the  Hong  Kong Federation of Fujian Associations ( 香港福建社團聯會 ). Mr. Chau was awarded a Bronze Bauhinia Star and a Silver Bauhinia Star by the Hong Kong government in 2010 and 2016, respectively.

## Mr. Radius Suhendra

Mr.  Radius Suhendra, aged 49, was appointed as an executive Director  of  the  Company  on  21  October  2016.  Mr.  Suhendra is  mainly  responsible  for  all  major  affairs  of  an  Indonesiaincorporated subsidiary of the Company, which was acquired by the Group in late 2016. He has been overseeing the construction and  operation  of  the  palm  kernel  oil  mill  and  the  ancillary biomass power plants in that subsidiary.

He  obtained  a  bachelor's  degree  in  business  administration from Ohio State University, the US, in 1996. He has 15 years of experience in managing businesses in heavy industries in Asia, including  7  years  in  managing  ferrous  trading  business  of  a ferrous alloy trading firm between 1998 and 2005 in Singapore. From 2006 to 2010, Mr. Suhendra was the president director of PT. Indoferro which operates blast furnace producing nickel pig iron in Indonesia.

陳先生於⼆零零四年十⼆月獲美國愛 荷華州立大學 （ Iowa  State  University  of Science  and  Technology ） 頒發理學學士 學位。彼為中國人民政治協商會議第 十⼆屆福建省委員會委員、香港福建社 團聯會第十屆董事會常務會董、香港閩 西聯會第十屆監事長、香港龍岩同鄉會 第十⼀屆監督委員會副會長及香港華僑 華人總會青年委員會第⼆屆主席。陳先 生為本公司⾮執行董事陳栢林先生的胞 兄。

陳 先 生 為 本 公 司 控 股 股 東 Everbest Environmental  Investment  Limited （ 「 Everbest  Environmental 」 ） 的 唯 ⼀ 董 事，並持有其 20% 的股權。陳先生亦擁 有 Kingdrive  Limited （ 「 Kingdrive 」 ） 20% 的 已發行股份。 Kingdrive 擁有 Carlton  Asia Limited 全部已發行股份，而 Carlton  Asia Limited 擁有本公司約 1.42% 的已發行股 份。

## 周致人先生

(' Exuberant '),  a company  incorporated  in  Hong  Kong,  which  was  intended  by the  then  shareholders  of  Everbest  Environmental  to  be  used as  the  holding  company  for  the  purpose  of  the  listing  of  the shares  of  the  Company  on  the  Stock  Exchange. Ng  (CPA)  Limited (previously  known  as  Tseung  &amp;  Ng  (CPA)  Limited)  since  April 2012,  prior  to  which  she  was  the  sole  proprietor  of  Linda  C.Y. ## Ms. Leung Bo Yee Nancy

Ms. Leung Bo Yee Nancy, aged 50, was appointed as an INED with  effect  from  15  October  2019.  She  has  been  a  member  of each of the Audit Committee, the Remuneration Committee and the Nomination Committee since 15 October 2019.

林先生持有香港理工大學會計學文學學 士學位，並獲得香港科技大學工商管理 碩士學位。彼是香港會計師公會會員。

## 汪自⼒先生

汪自⼒先生， 60 歲，於⼆零⼀四年三月 十八日獲委任為海安恆發設施副總經理。 汪先生於⼆零零五年⼆月加入本集團，主 要負責監督海安恆發設施相關營運事宜。 於加入本集團前，汪先生曾擔任北京瑞 權營養工程發展有限公司副主席及總經 理、中國青少年社會服務中心副總經理、 鐵道部株洲電⼒機車研究院研究員以及 曾於深圳市人才服務公司任職。汪先生 於⼀九八三年七月獲中國大連鐵道學院 （現 稱大連交通大學） 頒發工程學士學位。

## Corporate Governance Report 企業管治報告

The Company is committed to fulfilling its responsibilities to the Company's  shareholders  (the  ' Shareholders ')  and  protecting and  enhancing  Shareholder  value  through  good  corporate governance.

The  directors  of  the  Company  (the  ' Directors ')  recognise the  importance  of  incorporating  elements  of  good  corporate governance  in  the  management  structures  and  internal  control procedures  of  the  Company  and  its  subsidiaries  (the  ' Group ') so  as  to  achieve  effective  accountability,  and  to  enable  our Shareholders  to  evaluate  how  the  principles  of  the  CG  Code (defined below) have been applied.

## CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES

The  Company  has  applied  the  principles  and  complied  with all  the  mandatory  disclosure  requirements  and  the  applicable code  provisions  as  set  out  in  the  section  headed  'Part  2  Principles  of  good  corporate  governance,  code  provisions  and recommended  best  practices'  of  the  Corporate  Governance Code  contained  in  Appendix  14  (the  ' CG Code ')  to  the  Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the ' Listing Rules ') throughout the Year.

## SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS BY DIRECTORS

The  Company  has  adopted  the  Model  Code  for  Securities Transactions  by  Directors  of  Listed  Issuers  as  contained  in Appendix 10 to the Listing Rules (the ' Model Code ') as its own code  of  conduct  governing  the  securities  transactions  by  the Directors.  Following  a  specific  enquiry  made  by  the  Company on  each  of  the  Directors,  all  of  them  confirmed  that  they  had complied with the required standard set out in the Model Code during the Year.

本公司致⼒履行其對本公司股東 （ 「 股東 」 ） 之職責，並透過良好企業管治維護及提 高股東價值。

本公司董事 （ 「 董事 」 ） 深 信 要 達 至有 效 的 問責制度及確保我們的股東能夠評估應 ⽤企業管治守則 （定義見下文） 原則的方 式，在本公司及其附屬公司 （ 「 本集團 」 ） 之 管理架構及內部管控程序中引進良好企 業管治的元素⾮常重要。

## 企業管治慣例

本公司於本年度應聯交所證券上市規則 （ 「 上 市規則 」 ） 附錄十四 （ 「 企業管治守則 」 ） 中的 「第⼆部分 -良好企業管治原則、守則 條文及建議的最佳做法」 ⼀節所載原則， 並遵守了所有強制性披露要求及適⽤的 守則條文。

## 董事進行證券交易

本公司已採納上市規則附錄十所載之上 市發行人董事進行證券交易之標準守則 （ 「 標準守則 」 ） ，作為董事進行證券交易之 操守準則。經本公司向各董事作出具體 查詢後，彼等確認彼等於本年度⼀直遵 守標準守則所規定之準則。

A strong culture enables the Company to deliver  long-term  sustainable  performance  and  fulfil  its  role  as a  responsible  corporate  citizen.  The  Company  is  committed  to developing a positive and progressive culture that is built on its purpose, value and strategy.

The  Company  prides  itself  on  keeping  its  Shareholders engaged in and informed of the markets and communities in which the Company operates.

## Mutual Respect

The  Company  strives  for  working  internally  and  externally in  a  collegiate  environment  based  on  trust,  collaboration and respect.
